        "import torch\n",
        "import torch.nn as nn\n",
        "import torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "import pickle\n",
        "\n",
        "from torch_geometric.nn import (\n",
        "    GATConv,\n",
        "    GCNConv,\n",
        "    global_mean_pool,\n",
        "    SAGPooling\n",
        ")"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "E1SNL3cqcN9z"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": []
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# =====================================================\n",
        "# LOAD DATA\n",
        "# =====================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "with open(\"/content/proteinGraphsIndexed.pkl\", \"rb\") as f:\n",
        "    protein_graphs = pickle.load(f)\n",
        "\n",
        "# USE ONLY 1/5 DATA\n",
        "protein_graphs = protein_graphs[:1500]\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Loaded protein graphs:\", len(protein_graphs))\n",
        "\n",
        "# =====================================================\n",
        "# LOAD INDEXED PPI EDGES\n",
        "# =====================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "edges = []\n",
        "\n",
        "with open(\"/content/positiveEdges_indexed.csv\") as f:\n",
        "\n",
        "    next(f)\n",
        "\n",
        "    for line in f:\n",
        "\n",
        "        a, b = line.strip().split(\",\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        a = int(a)\n",
        "        b = int(b)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # keep only subset-safe edges\n",
        "        if a < len(protein_graphs) and b < len(protein_graphs):\n",
        "\n",
        "            edges.append([a, b])\n",
        "            edges.append([b, a])\n",
        "\n",
        "edge_index = torch.tensor(\n",
        "    edges,\n",
        "    dtype=torch.long\n",
        ").t().contiguous()\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Edge Index Shape:\", edge_index.shape)"
      ],

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# =====================================================\n",
        "# GAT1 MODEL\n",
        "# =====================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "class GAT1(nn.Module):\n",
        "\n",
        "    def __init__(self, input_dim=24, hidden_dim=512, heads=1):\n",
        "\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.fc1 = nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.conv1 = GATConv(\n",
        "            hidden_dim,\n",
        "            hidden_dim,\n",
        "            heads=heads,\n",
        "            edge_dim=1\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.conv2 = GATConv(\n",
        "            hidden_dim,\n",
        "            hidden_dim,\n",
        "            heads=heads,\n",
        "            edge_dim=1\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.conv3 = GATConv(\n",
        "            hidden_dim,\n",
        "            hidden_dim,\n",
        "            heads=heads,\n",
        "            edge_dim=1\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.pool1 = SAGPooling(hidden_dim)\n",
        "        self.pool2 = SAGPooling(hidden_dim)\n",
        "        self.pool3 = SAGPooling(hidden_dim)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.bn1 = nn.BatchNorm1d(hidden_dim)\n",
        "        self.bn2 = nn.BatchNorm1d(hidden_dim)\n",
        "        self.bn3 = nn.BatchNorm1d(hidden_dim)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, data):\n",
        "\n",
        "        # MINIMAL FIX\n",
        "        # batch does not exist in your dataset\n",
        "\n",
        "        x = data.x\n",
        "        edge_index = data.edge_index\n",
        "        edge_attr = data.edge_attr\n",
        "\n",
        "        # dummy batch for single graph\n",
        "        batch = torch.zeros(\n",
        "            x.size(0),\n",
        "            dtype=torch.long,\n",
        "            device=x.device\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        x = self.fc1(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # ---------------- Layer 1 ----------------\n",
        "\n",
        "        x_res = x\n",
        "\n",
        "        x = self.conv1(\n",
        "            x,\n",
        "            edge_index,\n",
        "            edge_attr=edge_attr\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        x = self.bn1(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "        x = F.relu(x + x_res)\n",
        "\n",
        "        x, edge_index, edge_attr, batch, _, _ = self.pool1(\n",
        "            x,\n",
        "            edge_index,\n",
        "            edge_attr=edge_attr,\n",
        "            batch=batch\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # ---------------- Layer 2 ----------------\n",
        "\n",
        "        x_res = x\n",
        "\n",
        "        x = self.conv2(\n",
        "            x,\n",
        "            edge_index,\n",
        "            edge_attr=edge_attr\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        x = self.bn2(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "        x = F.relu(x + x_res)\n",
        "\n",
        "        x, edge_index, edge_attr, batch, _, _ = self.pool2(\n",
        "            x,\n",
        "            edge_index,\n",
        "            edge_attr=edge_attr,\n",
        "            batch=batch\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # ---------------- Layer 3 ----------------\n",
        "\n",
        "        x_res = x\n",
        "\n",
        "        x = self.conv3(\n",
        "            x,\n",
        "            edge_index,\n",
        "            edge_attr=edge_attr\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        x = self.bn3(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "        x = F.relu(x + x_res)\n",
        "\n",
        "        x, edge_index, edge_attr, batch, _, _ = self.pool3(\n",
        "            x,\n",
        "            edge_index,\n",
        "            edge_attr=edge_attr,\n",
        "            batch=batch\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        protein_embedding = global_mean_pool(x, batch)\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"\\n[1,512] embedding shape:\")\n",
        "        print(protein_embedding.shape)\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"Sample values:\")\n",
        "        print(protein_embedding[0][:10])\n",
        "\n",
        "        return protein_embedding"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "1GuPHVkelDJH"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": []
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# =====================================================\n",
        "# JOINT GAT + GCN\n",
        "# =====================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "class JointGAT_GCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "\n",
        "    def __init__(self):\n",
        "\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.gat = GAT1()\n",
        "\n",
        "        # 512 -> 64\n",
        "        self.project = nn.Linear(512, 64)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # PPI GCN\n",
        "        self.gcn1 = GCNConv(64, 64)\n",
        "        self.gcn2 = GCNConv(64, 64)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, protein_graphs, edge_index):\n",
        "\n",
        "        device = edge_index.device\n",
        "\n",
        "        embeddings = []\n",
        "\n",
        "        # ---- GAT FOR EACH PROTEIN ----\n",
        "\n",
        "        for i, g in enumerate(protein_graphs):\n",
        "\n",
        "            g = g.to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "            emb = self.gat(g)         # [1,512]\n",
        "\n",
        "            emb = self.project(emb)   # [1,64]\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(\"\\nProjected [1,64] embedding:\")\n",
        "            print(emb[0][:10])\n",
        "\n",
        "            embeddings.append(emb)\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(f\"\\nProcessed Protein {i+1}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        # FINAL MATRIX\n",
        "\n",
        "        X = torch.cat(embeddings, dim=0)\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"\\nConcatenated X shape:\")\n",
        "        print(X.shape)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # ---- PPI GCN ----\n",
        "\n",
        "        X = F.relu(self.gcn1(X, edge_index))\n",
        "\n",
        "        X = self.gcn2(X, edge_index)\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"\\nFinal GCN Output Shape:\")\n",
        "        print(X.shape)\n",
        "\n",
        "        return X"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "6KOfUKp6lYpM"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": []
    },

              "    (conv3): GATConv(512, 512, heads=1)\n",
              "    (pool1): SAGPooling(GraphConv, 512, ratio=0.5, multiplier=1.0)\n",
